customer_success_rates = Customers.WHERE(
    COUNT(transactions_made) >= 5
).CALCULATE(
    customer_name=name,
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_made.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_transactions=COUNT(transactions_made)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_name=customer_name,
    success_rate=100.0 * successful_transactions / total_transactions
).ORDER_BY(success_rate.ASC())

# Define the list of FAANG ticker symbols for clarity
faang_tickers = ('AMZN', 'AAPL', 'GOOGL', 'META', 'NFLX')

# Filter customers based on two conditions:
# 1. Their email ends with '.com' (case-insensitive).
# 2. They have at least one transaction involving a FAANG stock.
faang_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (ENDSWITH(LOWER(email), '.com')) &
    (HAS(transactions_made.WHERE(ISIN(ticker.symbol, faang_tickers))) == 1)
)

# Count the number of distinct customers that match the criteria.
# Since we started from the Customers collection, each record in 'faang_customers' is unique.
result = GRAPH.CALCULATE(
    distinct_customer_count=COUNT(faang_customers)
)

# Define the list of FAANG ticker symbols for clarity and reusability.
faang_tickers = ('AMZN', 'AAPL', 'GOOGL', 'META', 'NFLX')

# The main query to count the customers.
# It starts by filtering the 'Customers' collection based on two conditions:
# 1. The customer's email address ends with '.com'. This is checked using ENDSWITH and LOWER for case-insensitivity.
# 2. The customer has at least one transaction involving a FAANG stock. This is verified using HAS() on the 'transactions_made' subcollection,
#    which is filtered to include only transactions where the ticker symbol is in the 'faang_tickers' list.
# Finally, COUNT() is used on the filtered customer list to get the total number of distinct customers who meet the criteria.
# The result is calculated at the top-level 'Broker' collection.
faang_customers_count = Broker.CALCULATE(
    num_customers=COUNT(
        Customers.WHERE(
            (ENDSWITH(LOWER(email), '.com')) &
            (HAS(transactions_made.WHERE(ISIN(ticker.symbol, faang_tickers))) == 1)
        )
    )
)

# 1. The customer's email address ends with '.com'. This is checked using ENDSWITH and LOWER for case-insensitivity.

# Define the start and end of the 8-week period, excluding the current week.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter for stock transactions within the specified date range.
recent_stock_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < end_of_period)
)

# For each transaction, determine its week and whether it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = recent_stock_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_week = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group transactions by week and count the total and weekend transactions.
weekly_summary = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=transaction_week).CALCULATE(
    week = transaction_week,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

result = weekly_summary

# First, calculate the date range for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# We need to go back 9 weeks and exclude the current week
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    # Filter for transactions in the last 9 weeks (we'll exclude current week later)
    DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week offset from now (0 = current week, 1 = last week, etc.)
    weeks_ago = DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")),
    # Determine if transaction happened on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
).WHERE(
    # Exclude current week (weeks_ago = 0)
    weeks_ago > 0
)

# Now partition by week and calculate counts
weekly_stats = transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=weeks_ago
).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = weeks_ago,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(weeks_ago.ASC())

result = weekly_stats

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-8 weeks')

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
transactions_in_range = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# Calculate the start date of the week for each transaction and identify if it's a weekend.
transactions_with_week_info = transactions_in_range.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition the transactions by the week's start date and calculate the total and weekend transaction counts.
result = transactions_with_week_info.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=(week_start_date)).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start_date,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.DESC())

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-8 weeks')

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
stock_transactions_in_period = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# For each transaction, calculate the start of its week and determine if it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = stock_transactions_in_period.CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group the transactions by the start of the week.
weekly_transaction_groups = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start)

# For each weekly group, count the total transactions and the number of weekend transactions.
result = weekly_transaction_groups.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(Transactions.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.ASC())

# Define the start and end of the 8-week period, excluding the current week.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter for stock transactions within the specified date range.
recent_stock_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < end_of_period)
)

# For each transaction, determine its week and whether it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = recent_stock_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_week = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group transactions by week and count the total and weekend transactions.
weekly_summary = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=transaction_week).CALCULATE(
    week = transaction_week,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

result = weekly_summary

# Define the time window: the start of the current week and the start of the 8th week prior.
# This is done at the top-level 'Broker' collection for clarity and to make these values available downstream.
time_boundaries = Broker.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_8_weeks_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
)

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the defined 8-week period.
# For each transaction, calculate the start date of its week, which will be used for grouping.
stock_transactions_in_period = time_boundaries.Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_8_weeks_ago) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week')
)

# Partition the filtered transactions into groups, with each group representing a unique week.
weekly_transaction_groups = stock_transactions_in_period.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=(week_start_date))

# For each weekly group, calculate the total number of transactions and the count of transactions that occurred on a weekend.
# A weekend is defined as Saturday or Sunday using the DAYNAME function.
result = weekly_transaction_groups.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start_date,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))))
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.ASC())

# First, calculate the date range for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# We need to go back 9 weeks and exclude the current week
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    # Filter for transactions in the last 9 weeks (we'll exclude current week later)
    DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week offset from now (0 = current week, 1 = last week, etc.)
    weeks_ago = DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")),
    # Determine if transaction happened on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
).WHERE(
    # Exclude current week (weeks_ago = 0)
    weeks_ago > 0
)

# Now partition by week and calculate counts
weekly_stats = transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=weeks_ago
).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = weeks_ago,

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-8 weeks')

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
stock_transactions_in_period = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# For each transaction, calculate the start of its week and determine if it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = stock_transactions_in_period.CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group the transactions by the start of the week.
weekly_transaction_groups = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start)

# For each weekly group, count the total transactions and the number of weekend transactions.
result = weekly_transaction_groups.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(Transactions.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.ASC())

# Define the start and end of the 8-week period, excluding the current week.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter for stock transactions within the specified date range.
recent_stock_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < end_of_period)
)

# For each transaction, determine its week and whether it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = recent_stock_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_week = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group transactions by week and count the total and weekend transactions.
weekly_summary = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=transaction_week).CALCULATE(
    week = transaction_week,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# Calculate the date range for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# Start of current week minus 8 weeks, end of current week minus 1 week
transactions_last_8_weeks = Transactions.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8)
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")),
    day_of_week = DAYOFWEEK(date_time),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition by week number and calculate counts

    name="weeks", 
    by=week_number
).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = week_number,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),

    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# First, calculate the date range for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# Current week starts from the most recent start of week
# We need to go back 9 weeks from now and exclude the current week
transactions_with_week_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    # Filter for transactions in the last 8 weeks (excluding current week)
    # This means from 9 weeks ago to 1 week ago
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week offset from now (1 = last week, 2 = two weeks ago, etc.)
    week_offset = DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")),
    # Determine if transaction happened on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = transactions_with_week_info.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_offset
).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = week_offset,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(weekend_flag = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).weekend_flag)
).ORDER_BY(weeks_ago.ASC())

# First, calculate the date range for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# We need to go back 9 weeks and exclude the current week
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    # Filter for transactions in the last 9 weeks (we'll exclude current week later)
    DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week offset from now (0 = current week, 1 = last week, etc.)
    weeks_ago = DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")),
    # Determine if transaction happened on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
).WHERE(
    # Exclude current week (weeks_ago = 0)
    weeks_ago > 0
)

# Now partition by week and calculate counts
weekly_stats = transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=weeks_ago
).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = weeks_ago,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(weeks_ago.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q5] Code generated by claude:
# Get transactions from USA customers that occurred last week
usa_transactions_last_week = Customers.WHERE(
    LOWER(country) == LOWER("USA")
).transactions_made.WHERE(
    (date_time >= DATETIME("now", "start of week", "-7 days")) &
    (date_time < DATETIME("now", "start of week"))
)

# Calculate the count and total amount
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    num_transactions=COUNT(usa_transactions_last_week),
    total_amount=SUM(usa_transactions_last_week.amount)
)

[DEBUG] [Q5] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start and end of the previous week, assuming the week starts on Sunday.
# 'start of week' with '-7 days' gives the start of last week.
# The end of last week is the start of the current week.
last_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')
last_week_end = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter transactions to include only those from USA customers within the last week.
last_week_usa_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(customer.country) == 'usa') &
    (date_time >= last_week_start) &
    (date_time < last_week_end)
)

# Calculate the total number of transactions and their total amount.
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(last_week_usa_transactions),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(last_week_usa_transactions.amount)
)

[DEBUG] [Q5] Code generated by claude:
# Get transactions from USA customers that occurred last week
usa_transactions_last_week = Customers.WHERE(
    LOWER(country) == LOWER('USA')
).transactions_made.WHERE(
    # Start of current week (Sunday)
    (date_time < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
    &
    # Start of last week (Sunday)
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'))
)

# Calculate the count and total amount
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    num_transactions=COUNT(usa_transactions_last_week),
    total_amount=SUM(usa_transactions_last_week.amount)
)

[DEBUG] [Q5] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start of the current week, assuming the week starts on Sunday.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Define the start of the previous week by subtracting 7 days.
start_of_last_week = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-7 days')

# Filter transactions to include only those from USA customers made last week.
last_week_usa_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(customer.country) == 'usa') &
    (date_time >= start_of_last_week) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# Calculate the number of transactions and the total transaction amount from the filtered set.
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(last_week_usa_transactions),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(last_week_usa_transactions.amount)
)

[DEBUG] [Q5] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start of the current week, noting that the week starts on Sunday.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Define the start of the previous week by subtracting 7 days from the start of the current week.
start_of_previous_week = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-7 days')

# Filter transactions to include only those from customers in the USA that occurred last week.
# The date range is from the start of last week up to (but not including) the start of the current week.
last_week_usa_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(customer.country) == 'usa') &

# Define the start and end of the previous week, assuming the week starts on Sunday.
# 'start of week' with '-7 days' gives the start of last week.
# The end of last week is the start of the current week.
last_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')
last_week_end = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter transactions to include only those from USA customers within the last week.
last_week_usa_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(customer.country) == 'usa') &
    (date_time >= last_week_start) &
    (date_time < last_week_end)
)

# Calculate the total number of transactions and their total amount.
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(last_week_usa_transactions),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(last_week_usa_transactions.amount)

# Filter transactions for buy transactions in the past 10 days
recent_buy_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'buy') & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
)

# Group by ticker symbol and count transactions
ticker_buy_counts = recent_buy_transactions.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker.symbol
).PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=ticker_symbol).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker_symbol,
    num_buy_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 2 most frequently bought tickers
result = ticker_buy_counts.TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q7] Code generated by gemini:
top_bought_tickers = Transactions.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10) & (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'buy')
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
).PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups", by=(ticker_symbol)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    num_buy_transactions=COUNT(Transactions)
).TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

# and calculate the ticker symbol for each.
recent_buy_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'buy') & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
).CALCULATE(

)

[DEBUG] [Q7] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only 'buy' types within the last 10 days.
recent_buy_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'buy') &
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
)

# Step 2: Create a new field for the ticker symbol to use it as a partition key.
transactions_with_symbol = recent_buy_transactions.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 3: Partition the filtered transactions by the ticker symbol and count the number of transactions in each group.
frequent_stocks = transactions_with_symbol.PARTITION(
    name="stocks", by=ticker_symbol
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    num_buy_transactions=COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Step 4: Select the top 2 stocks with the highest number of buy transactions.
top_2_most_frequent_stocks = frequent_stocks.TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

    (LOWER(transaction_type) == LOWER('buy')) & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
)

).PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=ticker_symbol).CALCULATE(

    num_buy_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 2 most frequently bought tickers
result = ticker_buy_counts.TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months', 'start of month')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(

)

[DEBUG] [Q8] Code generated by claude:
# Get customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# Calculate the date range: from 6 months ago to the start of current month
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months', 'start of month')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by join month to get customer signups per month
monthly_signups = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers)
)

# For PMAT, we need to calculate average transaction amount per month
# Get all transactions from these recent customers
customer_transactions = recent_customers.transactions_made.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(date_time), LPAD(MONTH(date_time), 2, '0')),
    transaction_amount = amount
)

# Partition transactions by month and calculate average
monthly_transactions = customer_transactions.PARTITION(name='tx_months', by=transaction_month).CALCULATE(
    month = transaction_month,
    pmat = AVG(transactions_made.amount)
)

# Combine the results - we need to join PMCS and PMAT by month
# Since we need both metrics for each month, let's use a different approach
# Calculate both metrics in one query by partitioning customers by join month

result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='signup_months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Get customers who signed up in the last 6 months excluding current month
# First, let's calculate the date range
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    signup_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by signup month to calculate PMCS and PMAT

    month = signup_month,

# Filter daily prices for the last 7 days (inclusive of today)
# and get the ticker type from the related ticker
recent_prices = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date, DATETIME('now')) <= 6  # 0-6 days ago = 7 days total
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type,
    close = close
)

# Partition by ticker type and calculate average closing price
result = recent_prices.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_types", 
    by=ticker_type
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    average_closing_price = AVG(DailyPrices.close)
)

# First, filter the DailyPrices to include only records from the last 7 days, inclusive of today.
# Then, create a new field 'type_of_ticker' to hold the ticker_type from the related Tickers collection.
recent_daily_prices = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date, DATETIME('now')) <= 6
).CALCULATE(
    type_of_ticker=ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition the filtered data by 'type_of_ticker' to group all records of the same type.
# For each group, calculate the ticker type and the average of the 'close' price.
acp_by_type = recent_daily_prices.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_type_groups", by=type_of_ticker
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_type=type_of_ticker,
    average_closing_price=AVG(DailyPrices.close)
)

    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    is_active_customer=IFF(LOWER(status) == 'active', 1, 0)
)

# Partition the filtered customers by country and calculate the components for the AR.

    name="by_country", by=(country)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    num_active_customers=SUM(Customers.is_active_customer),
    total_customers_with_transactions=COUNT(Customers)
).CALCULATE(

    AR=IFF(total_customers_with_transactions > 0, (num_active_customers / total_customers_with_transactions) * 100.0, 0)

# Step 1: Filter for customers who joined in 2022 and have transactions.
# A new field 'is_active_customer' is created to flag active customers (status is 'active').
customers_info = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    is_active_customer=IFF(LOWER(status) == 'active', 1, 0)
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered customers by country.
# For each country, calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) using the provided formula.
# The number of active customers is the sum of 'is_active_customer', and the total
# is the count of all customers in that group.
activity_ratio_by_country = customers_info.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    AR=(100.0 * SUM(customers_info.is_active_customer) / COUNT(customers_info))
)

# Filter customers who joined in 2022 and have at least one transaction.
customers_filtered = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
)

# Partition the filtered customers by country to calculate metrics for each group.
result = customers_filtered.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=(country)
).CALCULATE(
    # For each country, count the total number of customers and the number of active customers.
    country=country,
    active_customers_count=COUNT(Customers.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'active')),
    total_customers_count=COUNT(Customers)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) and select the final fields.
    # The IFF function prevents division by zero.
    country=country,
    AR=IFF(total_customers_count > 0, (100.0 * active_customers_count) / total_customers_count, 0)
)

# Filter customers who joined in 2022 and have at least one transaction.
customers_filtered = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
)

# Partition the filtered customers by country to calculate metrics for each group.
result = customers_filtered.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=(country)
).CALCULATE(
    # For each country, count the total number of customers and the number of active customers.
    country=country,
    active_customers_count=COUNT(Customers.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'active')),
    total_customers_count=COUNT(Customers)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) and select the final fields.
    # The IFF function prevents division by zero.
    country=country,
    AR=IFF(total_customers_count > 0, (100.0 * active_customers_count) / total_customers_count, 0)
)

# Step 1: Filter for customers who joined in 2022 and have transactions.
# A new field 'is_active_customer' is created to flag active customers (status is 'active').
customers_info = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    is_active_customer=IFF(LOWER(status) == 'active', 1, 0)
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered customers by country.
# For each country, calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) using the provided formula.
# The number of active customers is the sum of 'is_active_customer', and the total
# is the count of all customers in that group.
activity_ratio_by_country = customers_info.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    AR=(100.0 * SUM(customers_info.is_active_customer) / COUNT(customers_info))
)

[DEBUG] [Q12] Code generated by claude:
# Filter sell transactions from the past month
sell_transactions_past_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 30)
)

# Group by ticker symbol and calculate SPM
result = sell_transactions_past_month.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups", 
    by=(ticker.symbol,)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol,
    total_amount=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_tax=SUM(Transactions.tax),
    total_commission=SUM(Transactions.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    SPM=100.0 * (total_amount - (total_tax + total_commission)) / total_amount
)

[DEBUG] [Q12] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only 'sell' types from the last month (inclusive).
# Also, extract the ticker symbol to be used as a partition key.
sell_transactions_last_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol to group them.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_last_month.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=(ticker_symbol))

# Step 3: For each ticker group, calculate the total sell amount and total costs (tax + commission).
# Then, use a subsequent CALCULATE to compute the SPM using the provided formula.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(Transactions.tax) + SUM(Transactions.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - total_costs) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only 'sell' types from the last month (inclusive).
# Also, extract the ticker symbol to be used as a partition key.
sell_transactions_last_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol to group them.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_last_month.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=(ticker_symbol))

# Step 3: For each ticker group, calculate the total sell amount and total costs (tax + commission).
# Then, use a subsequent CALCULATE to compute the SPM using the provided formula.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(Transactions.tax) + SUM(Transactions.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - total_costs) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q12] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions to 'sell' types within the last month and extract the ticker symbol for grouping.
sell_transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol.
# For each ticker group, calculate the sum of amount, tax, and commission.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups",
    by=(ticker_symbol)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_tax=SUM(Transactions.tax),
    total_commission=SUM(Transactions.commission)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the final SPM using the aggregated values from the previous step.
# This includes a check to prevent division by zero if the total amount is zero.
# The final result includes only the ticker symbol and the calculated SPM.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - (total_tax + total_commission)) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

Recall that we want the symbol, and not just the ticker _id.

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    year = YEAR(date),
    month_num = MONTH(date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    year = year,
    month_num = month_num,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV to get previous month's average
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker"),
    momc = (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q13] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Create a base collection with the necessary calculated fields: ticker symbol and a formatted month string for grouping and sorting.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition the daily prices by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregates.
# This creates a collection where each record contains the stats for a specific ticker in a specific month.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly stats by symbol. This allows us to use the PREV window function
# to get the previous month's data for each ticker separately.
# The 'per="ticker_groups"' argument in PREV ensures the calculation is contained within each ticker's data.
result = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=(symbol)).monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="ticker_groups")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=average_closing_price,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's average is null or zero to avoid errors.
    MoMC=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (average_closing_price - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q13] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Prepare the daily price data by extracting the symbol and creating a sortable month string (YYYY-MM).
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregate statistics.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="ticker_months", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly statistics by symbol to create groups for the window function.
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: For each ticker group, calculate the month-over-month change (MoMC) using the PREV window function.
# The PREV function looks at the previous month's average close within the same ticker group.
result = ticker_groups.ticker_months.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    average_closing_price,
    highest_price,
    lowest_price,
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_month_avg_close != None) & (prev_month_avg_close != 0),
        (average_closing_price - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close,
        None
    )
)

# Step 1: Prepare the data by extracting the ticker symbol and creating a sortable month key.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_key=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Group by ticker and month to calculate the monthly aggregates.
monthly_aggregates = price_info.PARTITION(name="groups", by=("ticker_symbol", "month_key")).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    month=month_key,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly aggregates by ticker to create a window for each ticker.
# Then, within each ticker's window, calculate the MoM change using the PREV function.
result = monthly_aggregates.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=("ticker_symbol")).groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC())
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    momc=IFF(
        prev_month_avg_close > 0,
        (avg_close - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close,
        None

[DEBUG] [Q13] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Create a base collection with the necessary calculated fields: ticker symbol and a formatted month string for grouping and sorting.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition the daily prices by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregates.
# This creates a collection where each record contains the stats for a specific ticker in a specific month.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly stats by symbol. This allows us to use the PREV window function
# to get the previous month's data for each ticker separately.
# The 'per="ticker_groups"' argument in PREV ensures the calculation is contained within each ticker's data.
result = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=(symbol)).monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="ticker_groups")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=average_closing_price,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's average is null or zero to avoid errors.
    MoMC=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (average_closing_price - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

# Step 1: Prepare the data by extracting the ticker symbol and creating a sortable month key.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_key=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Group by ticker and month to calculate the monthly aggregates.
monthly_aggregates = price_info.PARTITION(name="groups", by=("ticker_symbol", "month_key")).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    month=month_key,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly aggregates by ticker to create a window for each ticker.
# Then, within each ticker's window, calculate the MoM change using the PREV function.
result = monthly_aggregates.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=("ticker_symbol")).groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC())
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    momc=IFF(
        prev_month_avg_close > 0,
        (avg_close - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close,
        None
    )
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly statistics by symbol to create groups for the window function.
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: For each ticker group, calculate the month-over-month change (MoMC) using the PREV window function.
# The PREV function looks at the previous month's average close within the same ticker group.
result = ticker_groups.ticker_months.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    average_closing_price,
    highest_price,
    lowest_price,
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_month_avg_close != None) & (prev_month_avg_close != 0),
        (average_closing_price - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close,
        None
    )
)

price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by ticker and month to calculate monthly aggregates

    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the aggregated monthly stats by symbol to facilitate the MoM calculation
# This creates groups where each group contains all monthly data for a single ticker
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: Within each ticker's group of months, calculate the MoMC
# Use the PREV window function to get the prior month's average closing price
result = ticker_groups.monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    # Get the previous month's average close, ordered by month, for each ticker
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's data is null or zero
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (avg_close - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

# Filter the daily prices to the specified date range from April 1, 2023, to April 4, 2023.
# Also, calculate the ticker_symbol to be used for partitioning.
filtered_daily_prices = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    (date >= '2023-04-01') & (date <= '2023-04-04')
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Partition the filtered data by ticker symbol to group all price records for each stock.
stocks_by_ticker = filtered_daily_prices.PARTITION(
    name="stocks", by=(ticker_symbol)
)

# For each stock group, calculate the price change by subtracting the minimum low from the maximum high
# over the period. Then, select the top 3 stocks with the largest price change.
result = stocks_by_ticker.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    price_change=MAX(DailyPrices.high) - MIN(DailyPrices.low)
).TOP_K(3, by=price_change.DESC())

# Partition the filtered data by ticker symbol to group all price records for each stock.
stocks_by_ticker = filtered_daily_prices.PARTITION(
    name="stocks", by=(ticker_symbol)
)

# For each stock group, calculate the price change by subtracting the minimum low from the maximum high
# over the period. Then, select the top 3 stocks with the largest price change.
result = stocks_by_ticker.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    price_change=MAX(DailyPrices.high) - MIN(DailyPrices.low)
).TOP_K(3, by=price_change.DESC())

    join_month=MONTH(join_date),
